- [ ] **AC-3** (FR-12a, b, e) A model with no price entry produces an `error` log naming provider, model, area and action, and is charged the **conservative documented fallback** — never $0; a test enumerates every model selectable in Layer 2's area settings and fails if any has no price; a per-image price resolves for every configured model / size / quality. *(SA-S2: the test enumerates every **code default**; DB-selected models are covered by DEC-7.)*
- [ ] **AC-4** (FR-12c, d) A fallback-priced row is flagged, counted in the operator report and countable from logs; reconciling it records an adjustment with its reason and leaves the original row intact.
- [ ] **AC-5** (FR-6) Credits are stored fractionally at the documented precision: an embedding-only action records a non-zero fraction, not 0 and not 1; the card and diary display rounded figures; and the diary for a period reconciles with the balance shown for that period under the documented rule.
- [ ] **AC-29** (FR-12g) *(added 2026-09-28)* The AI audit entry records the unrounded cost: an action whose only call is a ~$0.0000002 embedding shows a non-zero cost in its audit entry. A failed call on which the provider reported spent tokens is not recorded at $0, **or** the case is documented as a known issue with SA sign-off (SQ-16). The agent platform's charge for an identical agent run is unchanged before and after. *(Resolved by SQ-16: the "documented as a known issue with SA sign-off" branch applies — KI-8. Per SQ-14 the stored cost is rounded to 10 decimal places and the entry stays `schema: 1`; AC-29 is verified on the stored value.)*

**What is charged**

- [ ] **AC-6** (FR-7) A second identical image request (reuse cache) is charged **0** and does not move the balance; a chat turn served from the plan cache is charged only its lookup embedding — a small non-zero fraction (SA-S1).
- [ ] **AC-7** (FR-8) An action that threw after two paid calls is charged those two calls' cost and appears in the diary marked failed; an action with one failed call and a successful outcome is charged the whole action's cost, once.
- [ ] **AC-8** (FR-9) Writing the same invocation's charge twice produces no second row (the action id is unique); two onboarding turns in one conversation, and two chat turns sent with the same client header, each produce their **own** row; a retry that made new calls is charged for them.
- [ ] **AC-9** (FR-4, FR-10) Every declared action type has an audience, a setup flag, a charge rule and a diary label, asserted by a test that fails when a new type is added without them. A nightly insight run and a daily briefing are each charged to their own business with `trigger: 'scheduled'`.
- [ ] **AC-10** (FR-5) One action that made five LLM calls produces one row charged for all five; a fan-out over 12 records is charged its actual total, and the pre-check estimate is not what was charged.

**Ledger**

- [ ] **AC-11** (FR-13, FR-14) A ledger row carries the action id, `period_start`, the grouping id, the credits charged, the measured cost, the credit-value version, the fallback flag, the action type, the trigger (`owner` / `scheduled` / `external`) and the outcome — and no tokens, models, call names, areas or error codes; a code review finds no Business OS read or write of `user_subscriptions`, `credit_transactions` or `billing_events`, and no direct Supabase call outside `lib/repositories/`. *(Superseded wording, corrected by SQ-15: "A ledger row carries the action id, the credits charged, the measured cost, the credit-value version, the fallback flag, the action type, owner / automatic and the outcome".)* **Extended 2026-09-29 (BD-13 / BD-14):** the row also carries `service = 'ai'`, and its grouping id is never null. *(Refined 2026-09-29, SQ-18 / N-10: "the row" here is a **charge** row; an adjustment row carries a NULL `service` and inherits it.)*
- [ ] **AC-12** (FR-15, FR-16) With the audit queue stubbed to fail, the ledger row is still written; with the ledger write forced to fail, the owner's action still succeeds and one `error` log names the account, area, action type, grouping id and action id.
- [ ] **AC-13** (FR-17, FR-18) An account with 10 credits remaining and a 25-credit grant spends the 10 first; a new period resets consumption to zero and carries nothing over; a used-up trial total ends the trial.
- [ ] **AC-30** (FR-11) *(added 2026-09-28)* A code review finds that the charge row is built from `runAiAction`'s in-memory call list and that no charge path reads `token_usage`; the charge row's cost equals the unrounded sum of the action's per-call costs.
- [ ] **AC-34** (FR-13, FR-40a–c) *(added 2026-09-29, BD-12 to BD-14)* The ledger is neutral: the table, totals and record function are named `business_os_credit_charges`, `business_os_credit_totals` and `business_os_record_credit_charge`, and a code and migration review finds no `business_os_ai_*` ledger name; every row written by `runAiAction` has `service = 'ai'` and a non-null grouping id; a write with no grouping id is refused; a write with a new, well-formed `service` identifier (a test fixture, not a real service) is accepted **without a migration** and is counted in the **same** per-account, per-period total as the AI rows; a malformed `service` is refused; there is exactly one totals row per account per period. **Extended 2026-09-29 (SQ-18 ruling, N-10, C-6):** the format refused and accepted is exactly the table CHECK's (lowercase ASCII, letter first, then letters / digits / underscore, 1–64 characters) — e.g. `Ai`, `1ai`, `ai-x` and a 65-character id are refused; a charge row with a NULL `service` is refused; an adjustment row carries a NULL `service`, and the leak check and operator report count it under the **effective service** of the charge it adjusts; and a code review finds `'ai'` written from **one exported constant**, with no other `'ai'` service literal on the write path.

**At the limit**

- [ ] **AC-14** (FR-19, FR-20) With the allowance exhausted and mode `enforce`: a lead reply still goes out, with template text (the link chosen by the existing fixed rule, `pickFallbackCandidate` — C-1); a chat turn is refused with a normal assistant message that names the plan and the reset, and the conversation stays usable.
- [ ] **AC-15** (FR-21) With the ledger unreadable: an owner-facing AI action for an account last seen with credit left **runs** (BQ-1 option C); one last seen at zero is refused with the retryable `entitlement_unavailable` outcome, not "out of credits"; a client-facing automation runs; each case is logged at `error`. *(Superseded wording: "an owner-facing AI action is refused and logged at `error`".)*
- [ ] **AC-16** (FR-22) A turn refused by the daily chat budget and a turn refused by the period allowance produce different, correctly named refusals.
- [ ] **AC-17** (FR-23) Crossing 80% fires once in the period and not again; a setup regeneration that would exhaust a trial allowance warns before running and can be cancelled. In `shadow`, both are recorded and not sent.

**Owner surface**

- [ ] **AC-18** (FR-24, FR-25, FR-29) The card shows credits used and remaining against the resolved plan allowance, splits owner-triggered from automatic, and falls back to "consumption, no gauge" when no allowance resolves — with the diary still working.
- [ ] **AC-19** (FR-26) The diary shows time, area, plain-language action, trigger, outcome and credits per line, newest first, pages correctly, reconciles with the period's balance, and contains **no** prompt, owner text, model output, error message, token count or dollar figure. A request that tries to name another account's id gets its own data or a refusal, never the other account's. The diary reads only the charge table.
- [ ] **AC-20** (FR-27) With enough of an owner's own examples, a range is shown, labelled as their own past spend and never as a price; with too few, nothing is shown.
- [ ] **AC-21** (FR-28, FR-36) No owner-facing surface renders a token or dollar figure, and none derives an allowance from `monthly_ai_allowance_usd`.
- [ ] **AC-33** (FR-39) *(added 2026-09-28, BD-11)* With the dashboard open: after the owner runs an AI action, the card shows the new used / remaining figures **without a page reload**; after a scheduled run while the owner is on another tab, **returning to the tab** shows it; pressing the **refresh icon** shows a brief loading state and then the current figures; and with the page left idle, a network trace shows **no periodic requests** from the card. The figures match the period's running total for the owner's own billing period, not a rolling 30 days.
- [ ] **AC-35** (FR-24, FR-26, FR-40d) *(added 2026-09-29, BD-15)* No owner-facing surface — the card, the diary, their empty states or their refusal and warning messages, in en/he/es — calls the balance "AI credits"; the label is the neutral wording chosen in slice 6 / slice 7. With a fixture row of a non-`ai` service in the owner's period, the diary shows that line (under a generic label if the service declares none) and still reconciles with the balance.
- [ ] **AC-36** (R-7) *(added 2026-09-30)* **Until slice 6:** an owner's Settings → Plan and the public invite page show the plan's credit line as "Credits (included)" / he "קרדיטים (כלול)" / es "Créditos (incluido)" with **no number**, while the admin views show the figures. **After slice 6:** `withCustomerDisplay`, `SHOWN_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` and `INCLUDED_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` no longer exist in `lib/business-os/entitlements/customerPlanView.ts`, `creditsShownWithoutAmount.temporary.test.ts` is deleted, and every TEMPORARY-marked assertion listed in T6c of `docs/workplans/BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_SLICE_5_WORKPLAN.md` is restored.
- [ ] **AC-37** (FR-24, FR-25, FR-29, D-a to D-f) *(added 2026-09-30, slice 6 scoping — draft; the wording is final once the user decides D-a to D-f)* For a monthly plan the card shows the credits left of the plan's monthly allowance, the reset date of the owner's own billing period, and the credits used split "by you" / "automatic", the displayed parts adding up to the displayed total; for a trial it shows the one-off total, with no reset date and no "per month"; with no plan row it shows credits used with no gauge and never "0 of 0"; with nothing used it shows the full allowance and no split; over the allowance it shows 0 left and the true figure used, with no warning and no "paused" wording. The payload carries no token, dollar or cost field (an allow-list test).

**Operator**

- [ ] **AC-22** (FR-30) A grant and a reduction each: require an admin, reject an invalid body with 400, write one audit entry with actor, target, before/after and reason, and change the account's remaining balance. A non-admin, and a user aiming the operation at their own account, are both refused.
- [ ] **AC-23** (FR-31, FR-33) One account's allowance / used / granted / remaining and the deciding layer are readable without SQL, and the per-area, per-action-type, per-trigger report — with measured cost and the fallback-priced count — reproduces the same totals.
- [ ] **AC-24** (FR-32) An account whose plan has lapsed consumes no owner-facing AI, with no dependence on `account_frozen`.
- [ ] **AC-31** (FR-33, SA-S9) *(added 2026-09-28)* The nightly leak check, run against a test account with one deliberately unwrapped Business OS AI call and one forced ledger-write failure, reports both as uncharged spend with the account and period.
- [ ] **AC-32** (SA-S7) *(added 2026-09-28)* Past the per-account daily cap on stranger-triggered lead replies, further lead replies go out as template text and are not charged; the owner's own AI is unaffected.

**Allowances, day one and rollout**

- [ ] **AC-25** (FR-37) The credit value is recorded as configuration with its derivation; no plan or cohort allowance remains at a placeholder value; each carries its derivation (target cost, credit value, measured input, date); the entitlements doc and the capability's `unit` match what shipped. *(Note 2026-09-29: whether the capability id also changes from `ai.actions` to a general credit allowance is ruled by SA at slice 5 — SQ-17; AC-25 is checked against that ruling.)* **Made concrete 2026-09-30 (D1–D3):** the check passes when (a) `CREDIT_VALUE_HISTORY` gains **version 1** — $0.001, `status: 'derived'`, `decidedOn: '2026-09-30'`, a derivation string that names D1–D3 and points at the pricing doc — with version 0 byte-for-byte unchanged and `creditValue.history.json` extended in the same change; (b) the `basic` allowance reads **19,750** and the `pro` allowance **32,250** credits a month, and a Founding Partner resolves to **32,250** with no separate edit; (c) the trial's one-off total is the figure the user decides under **BD-16** (it may not stay at the 250 placeholder); (d) "each carries its derivation" is met by a pointer from `tierMatrix.ts`, `cohorts.ts` and `creditValue.ts` to [BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_PRICING.md](/docs/architecture/BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_PRICING.md), whose revision log holds the target cost, credit value, measured input and date; (e) the plan table in `BUSINESS_OS_ENTITLEMENTS.md` shows the same numbers; (f) the matrix version and the credit value's `matrixVersion` match SA's SQ-19 ruling. **Settled 2026-09-30 (later):** (c) means the trial's total is **`{ total: 2000 }`** — one-off, a whole number, with a test guarding a floor of at least 1,000 (BD-16); (f) means `TIER_MATRIX.version` is **2** and credit value version 1 carries **`matrixVersion: 2`** (SQ-19 ruled); and the capability is checked under its new id, **`credits.allowance`**, unit `'credit'` (SQ-17 ruled).
- [ ] **AC-26** (FR-34, FR-35) On the day charging starts, every account reads zero spent and an empty diary; no historical `token_usage` row is converted; no Pilot-Credit balance changes.
- [ ] **AC-27** (FR-38) In `shadow`, a fully exhausted account is refused nothing, while the ledger, the diary, the report and the warnings all work. Flipping to `enforce` is the only thing that changes behaviour.
- [ ] **AC-28** (end-to-end, non-production) On a test business: an onboarding conversation, an insight run, a briefing, a website generation, a lead reply, a chat turn, an image and one deliberately failed action each produce one diary line whose credit figure matches the measured cost; the card's figures before and after are recorded; the audit entry and the ledger row for each action carry the same **action id**. A concurrency test proves the same spend is never charged twice and records the measured overspend under a burst.

## 12. Slice plan

**Reorganised 2026-09-28, at the user's direction.** The rules for every slice:

- Slices are numbered **1, 2, 3 …** — no D-0 / D-1b labels.
- Each has a **title that says what it does**, and is **small enough to implement in a few days**.
- Each has a defined **scope** and explicit **guardrails** — what it must not touch.
- Every slice ships alone, leaves the product working, and runs in `shadow`: **no slice refuses anyone**. Switching enforcement on is S-5, outside this layer, and needs G-1.

The user proposed eleven slices. BA split the proposed "behaviour at zero" into two (slices 9 and 10) because, with SA-B2 and SA-B3 folded in, it would not fit in a few days: one slice builds the balance check, the other the pause-and-degrade behaviour and the missing template fallback. The rest are as proposed. *(Corrected by C-1: the lead-reply template fallback is not missing; slice 10 routes to it rather than building it, so slice 10 is smaller than first sized.)*

**BD-12 to BD-15 (2026-09-29) add no slice.** They change the ledger's names and shape in slice 3, the allowance naming question in slice 5 (SQ-17), and the owner wording in slices 6 and 7. No non-AI service is added to the plan. **The SA re-check of 2026-09-29 adds no slice either:** SQ-18's ruling and C-6 land in slice 3 (3b); N-10's effective service lands in slices 3, 4 and 7; N-11 is a rule for a future service.

**D1–D3 (2026-09-30) add no slice.** They answer BD-8 and fix slice 5's numbers; the trial total (BD-16) and the matrix version pairing (SQ-19) are settled inside slice 5.

**BD-16 and R-7 (2026-09-30, later) add no slice either.** BD-16 fixes slice 5's trial total (2,000 credits). R-7 puts a temporary display rule into slice 5 and a **required removal task** into slice 6.

**The slice 6 scoping (BA, 2026-09-30) adds no slice.** It details slice 6 and proposes splitting it into **6a** (the card reads credits; the temporary R-7 rule removed) and **6b** (the card refreshes right after the owner's own action; optionally the invite page in the invitee's language), both reaching owners with slice 7 — see [Slice 6 scoping](#slice-6-scoping-ba-2026-09-30).

### At a glance

| # | Title | Owner sees a change? | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | Label every AI action | No | — |
| **2** | No $0 cost | No | — (alongside 1) |
| **3** | Record every charge, silently | No | 1, 2 |
| **4** | Operator cost report and nightly leak check | No (operators do) | 3 |
| **5** | Set the credit value and plan allowances | No (the user decides) *(2026-09-30, R-7: owners and invitees see the plan's credit line as "Credits (included)", with no number, until slice 6)* | 4 |
| **6** | Owner usage card in credits | **Yes** | 3, 5 |
| **7** | Credit diary and typical range | **Yes** | 3, 6 |
| **8** | Warnings at 80% and 100%, and the setup pre-warning | Recorded, not sent, in `shadow` | 3, 4, 5 |
| **9** | Check the balance before every AI action | No | 1, 3 |
| **10** | Pause the owner's assistant, keep client work on templates | Only once enforced | 9 |
| **11** | Admin grant / reduce credits and per-account view | Admins do | 3, 9 |
| **12** | Guards before switch-on | Only once enforced | 9, 10 |

---

### Slice 1 — Label every AI action

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Every existing AI action type carries, in one place, the facts the charge and the diary need. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Nothing visible yet. It is the foundation: every future diary line will have a plain-language name in English, Hebrew and Spanish, and the product will know which actions face the owner and which face the owner's clients. |
| **Scope (in)** | On the **existing** `AiActionType` in `lib/business-os/llm/aiActionAudit.ts`, co-located with the type, a declaration for every type: area, audience (owner / client), setup flag, charged, diary label (en/he/es, or its key following the catalog's label pattern — the workplan chooses), template-fallback status (exists / missing / n/a). A guard test that fails when a type lacks an entry, inside the `typecheck:bos-llm` gate. The two dormant types included (KI-4). The fallback inventory: `lead_reply_recommendation` recorded as **`exists`** — every failure in `recommendLeadReply` falls back to `pickFallbackCandidate` (`LeadReplyRecommender.ts`, `leadReplyCandidates.ts`), a fixed rule with no AI call (C-1). Fix the stale "NOT WIRED YET" header (SA-S12). *(Superseded wording, corrected by C-1: "`lead_reply_recommendation` recorded as **missing** (OI-1)".)* |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | No separate, parallel list or module (user decision; supersedes SA's D-0 item 1 "one typed module next to `aiActionAudit.ts`"). No runtime behaviour change. No change to `runAiAction`'s behaviour, the audit `details` schema, `balance.ts`, `EntitlementService`, `ENFORCEMENT_POINTS`, any call site, the entitlements catalog's audience, or the `ai.actions` id. No pricing file. No migration, no UI. No change to the lead-reply fallback path — it is recorded, not touched (slice 10 routes "balance exhausted" to it). *(Superseded wording: "The missing fallback is recorded, **not built** (slice 10).")* `aiActionAudit.ts` is server-only, so slice 7 must resolve diary labels on the server (or ship keys) — never import this module into a client component (SA follow-up). |
| **Depends on** | Nothing. |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-4. AC-9 (the guard half). Also SQ-12, SA-S12, KI-4, OI-1 (closed by C-1), C-1. |

### Slice 2 — No $0 cost

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Fix the cost figure at its source, so `token_usage`, the audit entry and every future charge are right: never silently $0, never rounded away. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Nothing visible. It protects both sides: no action can ever be free by accident, and none can be over-charged without a flag. For Barak, the AI cost figures already on record become trustworthy. |
| **Scope (in)** | An unpriced model **fails loudly** (`error`, naming provider, model, area, action) and is never recorded as $0 (FR-12a). The **conservative fallback**, computed as the highest rate in the in-code table for the same provider and kind, flagged (FR-12b, SQ-9), via an optional price-source signal on `UsageCallRecord` set in `openaiProvider` (SA-S3), plus a pure Business OS function that slice 3 wires. **Price-coverage tests**: every code-default model priced > 0, every image default × size × quality resolves > 0, the default embedding model priced (SA-S2, FR-12e). The **audit entry stops rounding** to a micro-dollar and stores the unrounded cost (FR-12g, SQ-14). A **failed call recorded at $0 when tokens were spent** (SA N-3, `baseProvider.ts:166-176`) is assessed and fixed where the provider reports usage (SQ-16). *(Superseded by SQ-16: assessed, no code change — the provider reports no usage on an error; KI-8 accepted with SA sign-off.)* Optionally, the unpriced log level in `pricing.ts` goes from `warn` to `error`. **Folded in from SQ-13, SQ-14 and SQ-16 (SA, 2026-09-28):** (1) the per-call record (`UsageCallRecord`) carries the true figure plus the priced / unpriced signal, derived from a **separate read-only lookup** over the same price table — `calculateCost`, `calculateCostSync` and `resolveImagePrice` return nothing new; (2) `token_usage` **never** gets a Business OS-only figure — on an unpriced call, `token_usage` and the audit entry say **$0 with an `error` log**, and only the charge row (slice 3) carries the flagged conservative price; (3) an **absent** signal is **not** "unpriced" — the Business OS resolver re-checks the table with the same lookup and falls back only when the model is genuinely unpriced (this refines SA's D-0 item 4); (4) the audit cost is rounded to **10 decimal places**, not stored as a raw float, stays `schema: 1`, the tests that pin the old rounding change with it, and **no backfill** of older entries; (5) one named UI exception: the **admin audit-trail cost formatter** may become precision-aware (admin-only, no owner surface). Restated: **KI-8** (failed calls stay $0 — the provider reports no usage on an error; SA-accepted) and **KI-9** (OpenAI cached input is already priced at half rate in `openaiProvider.ts`). |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | **Must not change what the agent platform is charged**: `CreditService`, `pricingConfig`, `run-agent` and `chargeTokensWithIntensity` untouched, and an agent run costs the same credits before and after. Must not change what `calculateCost`, `calculateCostSync` or `resolveImagePrice` return for priced models (SA-S3; see SQ-13). No hand-typed fallback prices. Does not touch `anthropicProvider` or `kimiProvider` (`console.*` debt) unless the user approves converting them. No charge table, no migration, no UI, no call-site change, no change to `balance.ts`. **No change to the `baseProvider.ts` failure path** (SQ-16). The "no UI" rule has **one named exception**: the admin audit-trail cost formatter (SQ-14). |
| **Depends on** | Nothing; can run alongside slice 1. SQ-13, SQ-14 and SQ-16 **ruled 2026-09-28 and folded in above — the workplan may start.** *(Was: "Needs SA answers to SQ-13, SQ-14 and SQ-16 before its workplan.")* |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-11, FR-12a, b (mechanism), e, f, g, h. AC-3, AC-29. Also SQ-9, SQ-13, SQ-14, SQ-16, SA-S2, SA-S3, N-3, N-5, KI-8, KI-9. |

### Slice 3 — Record every charge, silently

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Every completed Business OS AI action writes one confirmed charge row, and each account has a running total for its period — while nothing is refused and nothing the owner sees changes. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Nothing visible. From the stated start moment, every AI action has a bill line behind it. This is the moment Business OS starts **counting** in credits. |
| **Scope (in)** | An **action id** minted per invocation inside `runAiAction` (SA-B1); the audit entry carries it (a `schema: 2` bump). The **thin charge table** (§4, FR-13) and the **per-account, per-period totals row** updated in the same transaction (SQ-1). The write is **direct, awaited with a short time budget, and never throws** (SQ-3), idempotent on the action id (SQ-2). The charge is built from the in-memory call list, never from `token_usage` (FR-11). **One resolver** converts cost to credits at a **provisional credit value, version 0**, in the entitlements config (FR-2, FR-3, SQ-7); slice 2's fallback flag is applied. Fractional storage (SQ-8). Failed actions charged what they spent (FR-8); background and external actions recorded with their trigger (FR-10). Table standards per SA-S8: FK to `auth.users`, owner-SELECT RLS only, server-write-only, repository access, registered as never-purged and in the deletion policy. A stated charging start; no history converted (FR-34, FR-35). **Row shape per SQ-15 (SA, 2026-09-28):** the thin row gains `period_start` (**required**, written at charge time; the totals row is keyed on it), `group_id` (**indexed, not unique** — the link to `token_usage.session_id`), and stores the trigger as **`owner` / `scheduled` / `external`** (slice 12's stranger cap counts `external`). **The adjustment-row shape is decided here** (a row kind plus a nullable reference to the adjusted action, or a sibling table), so slice 4 adds rows without a breaking migration; adjustment rows have their own id and never reuse an `action_id`. **Possible split** (SA follow-up): if the workplan's estimate exceeds a few days, split at the natural seam — **3a** action id + `schema: 2` + credit value v0 + the pure resolver (no DB); **3b** the tables, repository, RPC and wiring. *(Superseded wording: "background and external actions recorded as automatic (FR-10)".)* **A neutral ledger (BD-13, BD-14, user 2026-09-29) — applies to the 3b sub-slices, whose tables are not yet applied to production:** the charge table is **`business_os_credit_charges`**, the totals table **`business_os_credit_totals`**, and the record function **`business_os_record_credit_charge`** (renamed from the `business_os_ai_*` names, including in the migration, repository, RPC, tests, purge descriptor and deletion-policy registration); each row carries a **`service`** column — `'ai'` for every row written here, in an identifier format rather than a fixed list or enum (format per SQ-18); **`group_id` is required** (not null) on every row, still indexed and not unique; the totals stay **one row per account per period**, with no `service` in their key. **The `service` column per SA's SQ-18 ruling (2026-09-29):** required on **charge** rows and **NULL on adjustment rows**, which inherit it from the charge they adjust (N-10); a table **CHECK** — the single source of the format — of lowercase ASCII, a letter first, then letters, digits or underscores, 1–64 characters (the same rule as `action_type`); no enum and no registry, so a new service needs no migration; service ids are permanent and never reused. **3b writes `'ai'` from one exported constant** (SA condition **C-6**) — never a repeated literal, never from a request. Any read in this slice that selects or totals by service uses the **effective service** (the row's own, else that of the charge it adjusts). |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Refuses nothing, and adds no pre-check (slice 9). No owner surface. `balance.ts` stays `ALWAYS_SUFFICIENT`. No read of `token_usage` to build a charge. No tokens, models, call names, areas or error codes on the charge row. No row is ever updated in place. No grants table (slice 11); no adjustment rows **written** (slice 4) — only their shape decided. No Pilot-Credit table read or written. Must be **proven** unable to throw into, delay beyond its budget, or change the result of any action (SQ-11 condition 1, WC-21). *(Superseded wording: "no adjustment rows (slice 4)".)* **Added 2026-09-29 (BD-12, BD-13):** no non-AI service, price, wrapper or diary label is built; `service` is not restricted to a fixed list; no per-service totals row or per-service allowance; no `business_os_ai_*` ledger name survives. **Added 2026-09-29 (SQ-18, C-6):** no second definition of the `service` format outside the table CHECK; no `'ai'` service literal on the write path other than the one exported constant; no `service` value on an adjustment row. |
| **Depends on** | Slices 1 and 2. SQ-15 **ruled 2026-09-28 and folded in — the workplan may start.** *(Was: "Needs SA to confirm the thin row (SQ-15) before its workplan.")* SQ-18 (the `service` format) **ruled by SA 2026-09-29 and folded in above.** *(Was: "SQ-18 (the `service` format) to be settled in the slice 3b workplan or its SA code review.")* |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-1, FR-2, FR-3 (provisional value, versioning), FR-5 (charge side), FR-6 (storage), FR-7, FR-8 (charge side), FR-9, FR-10, FR-11, FR-12b (flag on the row), FR-13, FR-14, FR-15, FR-16, FR-18 (period totals), FR-34, FR-35, FR-38, **FR-40a–c, f**. AC-1, AC-2, AC-5 (storage), AC-6, AC-7, AC-8, AC-10, AC-11, AC-12, AC-26, AC-30, **AC-34**. Also SA-B1, SA-S1, SA-S8, SQ-15, **SQ-18**, **N-10**, **C-6**, **BD-12 to BD-14**. |

### Slice 4 — Operator cost report and nightly leak check

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Operators can see what AI costs us, per account, per action type and per trigger; and every night we check that no Business OS AI spend escaped the bill. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Owners see nothing. Barak and operators get the real numbers needed to set the credit value and the plan allowances, a count of charges that used a fallback price, and a nightly list of any spend with no charge behind it. |
| **Scope (in)** | The FR-33 report, readable without SQL: credits and cost per account and period, by action type, by area (through the slice 1 list) and by owner / automatic; the fallback-priced count (FR-12c); per-action-type spread (p50 / p90), which slice 8's pre-warning and slice 5's derivation use. The **nightly leak check**: Business OS spend in `token_usage` compared with the charge table, per account and period (SA-S9) — catches calls outside `runAiAction` and failed charge writes (FR-16). Reconciling a fallback-priced row by an **adjustment row** that points at the original (FR-12d). **Folded in from the SA follow-up (2026-09-28):** a **fallback-flagged row is not a leak** — its charge is above `token_usage` by design, so it is reported as reconciled-pending (SQ-13 (3)); the **drill-down** from a charge to its cost detail goes from the charge row's `group_id` to `token_usage.session_id` (SQ-15); and the check also covers calls **excluded from a usage scope** (`usageScope.ts:58-59`), the other source of uncharged spend. *(Added 2026-09-29, BD-13:)* the leak check compares `token_usage` with the **`service = 'ai'`** rows only; the report groups by `service` as well, so it stays correct when a later service writes rows. **Reworded 2026-09-29 by SA N-10:** adjustment rows carry no `service` of their own, so a plain `WHERE service = 'ai'` would skip them. The leak check compares `token_usage` with the rows whose **effective service** is `ai` — the row's own `service`, else the service of the charge it adjusts — and the operator report groups by **effective service**, so an adjustment is counted with the charge it corrects. The adjustment rows this slice writes carry a NULL `service` (SQ-18 ruling). |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Read-only over `token_usage` and the charge table, except the adjustment rows. No automatic correction and no backfill of missed charges — a backfill is decided once the loss rate is measured (SQ-3, N-7). No owner-visible change. No change to how `token_usage` is written. Admin access only through `requireAdmin`. A nightly job depends on `CRON_SECRET` being set on Vercel, or it stays dormant (fail-closed). *(Added 2026-09-29, N-10:)* No plain `WHERE service = …` filter or `GROUP BY service` that would drop or mis-bucket adjustment rows. |
| **Depends on** | Slice 3; a few weeks of shadow data before slice 5 uses it. *(Note 2026-09-30: slice 5 did not wait for a few weeks of shadow data. The user decided D1–D3 on a benchmark of ~10 days of audit and ledger data from two test accounts, because the chosen ceiling leaves ~330–540× headroom over it; the pricing doc's revisit triggers — first paying customers, 30+ days of real usage, any account above ~25% of its allowance — are where slice 4's report takes over.)* |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-12c, FR-12d, FR-16 (detection), FR-33, **FR-40f**. AC-4, AC-23 (report half), AC-31. Also SA-S9, N-7, SQ-13 (3), SQ-15, **N-10**. |

### Slice 5 — Set the credit value and plan allowances

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Replace the invented numbers with a real credit value and per-plan allowances derived from measured cost. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | **Barak decides one thing: how much AI cost we are willing to carry per plan per month** (BD-8). From that and slice 4's numbers, each plan gets a credit allowance that means something. Owners see nothing yet; slice 6 shows it. *(Answered 2026-09-30 — D1–D3: 25% of each plan's fee, at a 100% markup, as a generous ceiling. See §13 BD-8.)* *(2026-09-30, later — R-7, option C:)* until slice 6, owners (Settings → Plan) and invitees (the public invite page) see the plan's credit line as "Credits (included)" / he "קרדיטים (כלול)" / es "Créditos (incluido)" with **no number**; admins see the figures. |
| **Scope (in)** | A configuration change (FR-37): the credit value as a versioned entry in the entitlements config with its derivation and date (SQ-7, SA-S6); new `basic` / `pro` allowances and every `PLACEHOLDER` cohort quantity, including the trial's one-off total; the capability's `unit` and labels changed from action to credit; `docs/architecture/BUSINESS_OS_ENTITLEMENTS.md` updated. The credit value is chosen so that a typical chat turn is at least one whole credit (N-8). **Allowance naming (user, 2026-09-29):** the user wants the `ai.actions` capability to become a **general credit allowance** — the one pool of BD-12 — when allowances are set here, with neutral labels (BD-15). Whether that means renaming the capability id (and migrating the admin overrides that reference it) or keeping the id and neutralising only its unit and labels is **for SA to rule at the start of this slice** (SQ-17). **The numbers (decided 2026-09-30, D1–D3 — single source: [BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_PRICING.md](/docs/architecture/BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_PRICING.md)):** (1) **Credit value:** keep **$0.001**; append **version 1** to `CREDIT_VALUE_HISTORY` with `status: 'derived'`, `decidedOn: '2026-09-30'` and a derivation naming D1–D3 and pointing at the pricing doc; extend `creditValue.history.json` in the same change; **version 0 is not edited** (FR-3). N-8 is met: the benchmark measured a chat turn at ~$0.002, about 2 credits. (2) **Allowances:** replace `ai.actions` **500 → 19,750** (`basic`, Essentials $79) and **2,000 → 32,250** (`pro`, Autopilot $129) credits a month, from allowance = (fee × 0.50 ÷ 2) ÷ $0.001; Founding Partner (`champion`) reads the `pro` row and needs no edit. Both are **raises**, so no `removals` entry is needed under the entitlements rules; whether the matrix `version` is bumped anyway is **SQ-19** (SA). (3) **Trial:** the one-off total is **2,000 credits** (**BD-16**, decided by the user 2026-09-30) — one-off, not monthly; ≈ $2 maximum cost per trial; ≈ 6× the heaviest measured setup month; a test guards a floor of at least 1,000. D1–D3 do not derive it. *(Superseded wording, 2026-09-30: "(3) **Trial:** the one-off total is **BD-16** (pending the user); D1–D3 do not derive it.")* (4) **Derivation next to the numbers:** a pointer comment to the pricing doc in `tierMatrix.ts`, `cohorts.ts` and `creditValue.ts`, replacing the "FIRST PASS" / "PLACEHOLDER" wording for the numbers it covers — the reasoning itself is not copied into code. (5) **Docs:** the plan table in `BUSINESS_OS_ENTITLEMENTS.md` updated to the new figures, with a link to the pricing doc; the pricing doc's §1 "current configuration" and revision log updated when slice 5 ships. **What slice 5 ships (recorded 2026-09-30, SA finding CR-2):** `ai.actions` renamed to **`credits.allowance`** (unit **`'credit'`**; SQ-17 ruled by SA); tier matrix **version 2** (SQ-19 ruled by SA); credit value **version 1** (`derived`, **$0.001**, **2026-09-30**); **Essentials 19,750 / Autopilot 32,250 / Founding Partner 32,250 a month / trial 2,000 in total**. The numbers and their reasoning live in [BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_PRICING.md](/docs/architecture/BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_PRICING.md). **Temporary display rule (R-7, option C, user 2026-09-30):** `lib/business-os/entitlements/customerPlanView.ts` shows the credit line to owners and invitees as "Credits (included)" with no number (`withCustomerDisplay`, `SHOWN_WITHOUT_AMOUNT`, `INCLUDED_WITHOUT_AMOUNT`), pinned by the temporary test `creditsShownWithoutAmount.temporary.test.ts`, with the affected assertions marked TEMPORARY; admins see the figures. Removed in slice 6 (required task). **Not in slice 5:** the `console.*` logging in `lib/business-os/LanguageContext.tsx` was converted by a **separate change, merged as PR #155**. |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Config and documentation only — no code path changes. No recorded charge is rewritten (FR-3). The `ai.actions` id is not renamed (SA-S11). No tier may grant a `not_built` capability. No enforcement. *(Under review, 2026-09-29: the "id is not renamed" guardrail stands until SA rules SQ-17 at this slice; if SA rules for a rename, this guardrail is superseded by that ruling, and the migration of the override rows comes into scope.)* **Added 2026-09-30:** no edit to credit value version 0 or to its JSON history entry; no change to the credit value's number ($0.001 stays); no markup applied to the charge itself — the markup lives only in the allowance size (D1); no allowance **lowered** (both changes are raises; a lowering would need a `removals` entry and grandfathering); the numbers' rationale lives in the pricing doc only, not duplicated in code comments or this requirement beyond FR-37's summary. **Superseded 2026-09-30 by SA's SQ-17 ruling (slice 5 review):** the id **is** renamed, to `credits.allowance`; no override-row migration comes into scope, because SA measured 0 override rows on `ai.actions` (SA's condition C-3 re-counts before merge). SA's guardrails G-1 to G-10 for this slice are in the [slice 5 SA review](#sa-review--slice-5-2026-09-30). **Added 2026-09-30 (later), R-7:** the temporary option-C rule changes **what owners and invitees are shown** only — never a resolved value, and never what admins see; it is marked TEMPORARY in code and tests so slice 6 can find all of it. |

### Slice 6 — Owner usage card in credits

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | The dashboard card tells the owner the truth: credits used and left of their plan, split into what they asked for and what ran automatically — and it stays current without a page reload. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | One honest AI number on the dashboard, for example "620 of 1,000 credits used this period — 480 by you, 140 automatic — resets on the 14th". The old, display-only allowance disappears. The card updates right after the owner uses the AI, when they come back to the page, and when they press its small refresh icon (BD-11). *(2026-09-30, R-7:)* the plan screen and the invite page go back to showing the plan's credit figure, now that the owner can see what they have used against it. |
| **Scope (in)** | FR-24, FR-25, FR-28, FR-29, FR-36. The card reads the totals row and the charge table; the allowance comes from the entitlements resolver. Rounded display (FR-6). Labels in en/he/es. The display-only `monthly_ai_allowance_usd` path and the agent-platform rate-key reads are retired from the owner surface (closes the read side of TK-6, SQ-10). **Card refresh (FR-39, BD-11):** the card re-reads (a) right after the owner's own AI action finishes, (b) when the owner returns to the tab or page, and (c) from a small manual refresh icon on the card, with a brief loading state; no background or timer polling, so automatic work shows on the next refresh. **As-built today (A-18):** the card reads a rolling 30 days of `token_usage` at tokens ÷ 10, against a platform-wide allowance of $10 ÷ $0.00048 ≈ 20,800 credits regardless of plan, fetched once on page load. **After this slice:** it reads the charge table's running total for the owner's own billing period. **Neutral wording (BD-15, user 2026-09-29):** the card's heading and labels never say "AI credits"; the **final wording** — for example "Credits" or "Operating credits" — is **decided in this slice**, with the user, in en/he/es, and the same word is reused by slice 7. The card's figures are the one pool (every service's rows), which in this layer are all AI. **Required task — remove the temporary option-C rule (R-7, user 2026-09-30):** remove `withCustomerDisplay`, `SHOWN_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` and `INCLUDED_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` from `lib/business-os/entitlements/customerPlanView.ts`; delete the temporary test `creditsShownWithoutAmount.temporary.test.ts`; and restore the TEMPORARY-marked assertions (the `customerPlanView` tests, and the `PlanSection` render test including the synthetic cross-unit line). The exact list is T6c of `docs/workplans/BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_SLICE_5_WORKPLAN.md`. **Slice 6 is not done until this task is done** (AC-36, KI-17). |

Caller-scoped only, no account id in a request. No background or timer polling (BD-11). **BA recommendation:** slices 6 and 7 may be built separately, but should reach owners in the **same release** — under option B the diary is the owner's predictability, and a falling number with no explanation behind it invites support tickets (KI-11). *(Added 2026-09-29:)* No "AI credits" wording, and no per-service balance on the card. **Added 2026-09-30 (R-7):** must not ship with any part of the option-C rule, its temporary test or a TEMPORARY-marked assertion left in place. |

| Fact | Today | Source |
|---|---|---|

##### 1. What the owner sees

**The card, for a monthly plan (Essentials, Autopilot, Founding Partner).** Same place on the dashboard, same ring:

| Part | Content | Example (Founding Partner, period 14th → 13th) |
|---|---|---|
| Title | The neutral name (D-a) | "Credits" |
| Beside the title | The date the owner's own billing period resets (T-6), and a small refresh icon (FR-39 c) | "Resets 14 Oct" · refresh icon |
| Centre of the ring | Credits **left** of the plan allowance, with the allowance underneath | "32,187" / "left" / "of 32,250" |
| The ring | Empties as the period is spent, against the plan's allowance (not a platform-wide $10) | Almost full |
| Under the ring | Credits **used** this period, split "by you" and "automatic" (FR-25) | "63 used — 41 by you · 22 automatic" |
| Explanation | One sentence on what a credit is (D-b), small, under the figures or behind an info icon | "Credits measure the work Business OS does for you…" |

"By you" = charges whose trigger is `owner`; "automatic" = `scheduled` + `external` (FR-10). An adjustment counts under the trigger of the charge it corrects (SQ-22). "Used" is the period's running total, and the displayed parts add up to the displayed total (D-c). No tokens, no dollars (FR-28).

**The card, for the trial (Test Flight).** The same card, but against the trial's **one-off total**: "1,652 left of 2,000 in total", the same split, and **no reset date** — the label reads "For your trial" instead. No "per month" wording anywhere on a trial card. The trial's end date stays on the plan screen, where it already is.

**States.**

| State | What the card shows | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| Loading | The frame with "—", as today | — |
| Could not read | The existing short error line — never a zero, never internal detail in production | CLAUDE.md error format; "could not read" is never shown as "nothing used" |
| **No plan row**, or a plan that resolves no credit allowance | Credits **used** this period and the split, with **no gauge and no "left"** — what the card does today when no allowance applies | FR-29, AC-18. The resolver reports a withheld `{ perMonth: 0 }` for an account with no plan row; the card treats that as "no allowance", never as "0 of 0, all used" (D-e) |
| **Platform or internal account** (an operator's own or test account, e.g. the one the chat golden set runs on) | Whatever its own plan row resolves to — no special case; with no row, the no-plan-row state | BA reading; the owner surface should not know an account is internal (SQ-26) |
| **Nothing used** this period (e.g. the first day) | The full ring, "32,250 left of 32,250", and "Nothing used yet this period" instead of the split | Replaces today's `usage.none` line |
| **Near the allowance** | The same figures. The arc keeps the orange tint it already takes below 20% left; **no warning text, banner or notification** | Warnings are slice 8 (FR-23); in `shadow` they are recorded, not sent |
| **At or over the allowance** | "0 left" (never negative, §5) and the true figure used, e.g. "32,260 used of 32,250". **No "paused" wording** | Nothing is refused in `shadow` (FR-38); the pause message is slice 10 |
| Grants | Not shown — none exist until slice 11. The payload leaves room so slice 11 adds a line, not a new shape (SQ-27) | FR-17 |

**When it refreshes (BD-11, FR-39).** The card re-reads (a) right after an AI action the owner started from the page finishes — a chat answer, a briefing refresh, a website regeneration; (b) when the owner comes back to the tab or the page; (c) when the owner presses the refresh icon, which shows a brief loading state. No timer and no background polling. Automatic work (last night's insights, the morning briefing, a lead reply) appears on the next of these (KI-12). How (a) reaches the card from another component is SQ-24.

**The plan screen (Settings → Plan), once the temporary R-7 rule is removed (AC-36).**

| Plan | Included list | "Next plan up" |
|---|---|---|
| Founding Partner | "Credits (32,250 per month)" | None, as today (they are on Autopilot's row) |
| Autopilot | "Credits (32,250 per month)" | None (the top tier) |
| Essentials | "Credits (19,750 per month)" | Autopilot, which **improves** credits "19,750 per month → 32,250 per month", plus the chat features it adds |
| Trial | "Credits (2,000 in total)" | Essentials, where credits are a **change**, not an improvement: "2,000 in total becomes 19,750 per month" (different quantities, no "more / less" claim — tested before R-7) |

The trial's "Ends on {date}, or when the credits run out…" sentence is unchanged. "per month" and "in total" are English in every language today (`describeCapabilityValue`, pre-existing); see the optional fix in §3. The line sits under the "AI assistant" heading (`category: 'ai_chat'`, SA N-12) unless D-g moves it.

**The invite page (public `/invite`).** The offer shows the same line with its number: a Founding Partner invite "Credits (32,250 per month)", an Essentials invite "Credits (19,750 per month)", a trial invite "Credits (2,000 in total)". **This is the first time a prospective customer sees a credit figure** — the reason R-7 held it back. The page's plan list is English in every invite language today (slice 5 QA-2); localising it is optional here (§3, OI-10).

##### 2. Business decisions for the user (slice 6)

Labelled **D-a to D-g** for slice 6 only — not D1–D3 (BD-8) and not the old D-0 to D-4 slice names. Each has a BA recommendation; the user decides. Summary: §13, [Slice 6 decisions for the user (2026-09-30)](#slice-6-decisions-for-the-user-2026-09-30).

**D-a — the name owners see.** The balance is one pool for AI today and non-AI services later (BD-12), so the name must not say "AI" (BD-15).

| Option | en / he / es | For | Against |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. "Credits"** | Credits / קרדיטים / Créditos | Already the working label on the plan screen (slice 5 catalog); short; fits every future service | Generic, so it needs the one-sentence explanation (D-b) |
| B. "Operating credits" | Operating credits / קרדיטים תפעוליים / Créditos operativos | Says they pay for work, not AI only | Longer; a new term to explain; tight in the card title in he / es |
| C. "Usage credits" | Usage credits / קרדיטי שימוש / Créditos de uso | Says what they measure | Sounds like a meter to watch — the opposite of D3's generous ceiling |
| D. Keep "Pilot Credits" | — | No change for today's owners | That is the agent platform's own, separate currency (A-1, A-11); using it would tell owners two separate balances are one |
| ~~"AI credits"~~ | — | — | Ruled out by BD-15 |

**BA recommendation: A, "Credits".** Split labels: "by you" / "automatic" — he "על ידך" / "אוטומטי", es "por ti" / "automático". Slice 7's diary reuses the same word (BD-15).

**D-b — one sentence that explains a credit.** Draft; **he and es need native review** before release.

| | Monthly plan | Trial |
|---|---|---|
| en | "Credits measure the work Business OS does for you. Each task uses credits according to what it actually took to run, and your plan includes a fixed amount every month." | Same first sentence; ends "…and your trial includes a fixed amount in total." |
| he | "קרדיטים מודדים את העבודה ש־Business OS עושה בשבילך. כל משימה משתמשת בקרדיטים לפי מה שנדרש בפועל כדי לבצע אותה, והתוכנית שלך כוללת כמות קבועה בכל חודש." | Ends "…ותקופת הניסיון שלך כוללת כמות קבועה בסך הכול." |
| es | "Los créditos miden el trabajo que Business OS hace por ti. Cada tarea usa créditos según lo que realmente requirió realizarla, y tu plan incluye una cantidad fija cada mes." | Ends "…y tu prueba incluye una cantidad fija en total." |

Options: **A. the neutral sentence above**; B. a sentence with examples ("an assistant answer uses about 2 credits, a morning briefing less than 1"). **BA recommendation: A.** Examples with numbers read as prices, which option B of BD-1 deliberately does not give (KI-1); the owner's own examples come from the diary and the own-history range (slice 7, FR-27).

**D-c — whole credits or one decimal on the card.** A briefing ≈ 0.2 credit, a chat turn ≈ 2, a lead reply ≈ 0.08; a Founding Partner uses ≈ 60 a month against 32,250.

| Option | The card shows | For | Against |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. Whole credits; "less than 1" when between 0 and 1** | "63 used", "32,187 left"; after one briefing, "less than 1 used" | Clean at these sizes; never says "0 used" when something was used | One cheap action does not visibly move the number |
| B. Whole credits, plain rounding | "0 used" after two briefings | Simplest | Shows 0 when the ledger holds 0.4 — reads as "not counted" |
| C. One decimal | "63.4 used", "32,186.6 left" | Every action moves the number | False precision on a 32,250 allowance; decimal separators differ in he / es |

**BA recommendation: A on the card.** "Used" is the unrounded total rounded to a whole number (with the "less than 1" case); "left" = allowance − the **displayed** used, so the two visibly add up; "by you" and "automatic" are rounded so they add up to the displayed used (the exact rule is FR-6's documented rule, for SA). Diary lines are slice 7; BA's suggestion there is one decimal per line with the footnote "lines are rounded; the total is exact" (SQ-8).

**D-d — release with slice 7, or ship slice 6 first.**

| Option | What happens | For | Against |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. Release 6 and 7 together** | Slice 6's approved PR waits for slice 7's; they merge back to back (merging to `main` deploys) | KI-11: the diary explains every movement of the number; SA endorsed it (SA follow-up, slices 6 and 7) | "Credits (included)" stays on the plan screen a few days longer |
| B. Ship 6 first behind a feature flag | 6 merges, switched off in production until 7 | Smaller PRs reach `main` sooner | Old and new card, and the old and new plan-screen rule, must work side by side — the temporary R-7 rule becomes a conditional one, more to unwind; a flag for one release |
| C. Ship 6 alone, openly | Owners get the new card without a diary | Fastest. The audience is 8 friendly Founding Partners whose number barely moves (≈ 60 of 32,250 a month) | Automatic spend they cannot itemise yet; the card changes again when the diary arrives |

**BA recommendation: A.** If slice 7 slips by more than about a week, fall back to **C**, not B: the risk of C is small at today's audience and spend, and B puts a temporary switch on top of a temporary rule.

**D-e — what a Founding Partner, and an account with no plan row, see.**

| Case | Options | BA recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| Founding Partner (free, reads Autopilot's row) | A. "32,250 per month", like Autopilot; B. "Included" / "Unlimited", no number; C. the number on the card, "included" on the plan screen | **A.** The number is true (they carry Autopilot's ceiling, KI-15), it lifts R-7 as intended, and "unlimited" would be false. The plan screen already tells them their access is free with no end date |
| No plan row, or no resolvable allowance | A. Usage only, no gauge (FR-29); B. hide the card; C. show a default plan's allowance | **A**, which FR-29 already requires. Never a default the account does not have, never "0 of 0". Every live account has a row today (8 champions), so this is an anomaly state |

**D-f — the old number, on the day slice 6 ships.** Today every owner sees ≈ 20,833 Pilot Credits counting down over a rolling 30 days. After slice 6 a Founding Partner sees about "32,249 left of 32,250", with usage in the tens. The number goes **up**, changes unit and resets on a different date.

| Option | What owners see | For | Against |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. Replace it on deploy, with the D-b sentence on the card** | The new card from the first page load | One honest number (BD-7: making it true is a fix); nothing is charged or refused | A visible jump with no notice |
| B. A, plus a one-time "Your usage is now shown in credits" note, dismissed by the owner | As A, with a note the first time | Explains the jump inside the product | A per-owner "dismissed" state for a one-off event — a new UI pattern for SA |
| C. A, plus a personal note from the user to the current Founding Partners | As A | Personal; no code | Does not scale past friendly accounts |

**BA recommendation: A with C.** B is not worth a new pattern for 8 accounts.

**D-g — the heading the credits line sits under on the plan screen** (SA N-12). Today it is "AI assistant" (`category: 'ai_chat'`). Options: A. keep it (no work; wrong once a non-AI service draws from the pool); B. its own neutral heading, "Credits", first in the list, since it applies to everything below it; C. under "Platform". **BA recommendation: B.** It needs a heading in three languages and a category-order change (SQ-25), about a quarter of a day.

##### 3. Technical scope for SA (not decisions for the user)

1. **The route.** `GET /api/business-os/usage` (replaced in place, or a sibling — SQ-28) returns for the **caller only**: the period (its start and reset date, or "trial total"); the allowance, from the entitlements resolver's `credits.allowance` (`{ perMonth }` or `{ total }`; `null` when none resolves or the value is withheld); used, from the period's `business_os_credit_totals` row; the split by effective trigger, from the period's charge rows (SQ-22); remaining, clamped at 0. **No tokens, no dollars, no cost column** (FR-28, AC-21) — pinned by an allow-list test over the payload keys. `getUser()` first; Zod on any input; no account id accepted from the request; Pino with a `correlationId`; the CLAUDE.md error format.
2. **Repositories only** (rules 1 and 4). Either `BusinessOsCreditLedgerReadRepository` (read-only, account-scoped by signature, service role today) or a method on it called with the owner's RLS client (SQ-21). Owners cannot read cost columns (per-column grants, slice 3).
3. **The period** from the account's plan anchor (T-6), computed by the **same function** the charge recorder uses to stamp `period_start`, so the card reads exactly the totals row the charges went into (SQ-20). The trial's "used" spans every period since the trial began (SQ-20).
4. **The card** (`components/business-os/UsageCard.tsx`): §1's content and states; the three refresh triggers with no timer; the wording as dictionary keys in en / he / es in `lib/business-os/LanguageContext.tsx`, rendered on the client (the server sends facts — the rule the current route already documents); the "last 30 days" and Pilot Credits wording retired from the card.
5. **Retire the agent-platform rate keys from the owner surface** (FR-36, AC-21; SQ-10 / TK-6 read side): `readAllowanceCredits` (`monthly_ai_allowance_usd`, `pilot_credit_cost_usd`), `readTokensPerCredit` (`tokens_per_pilot_credit`) and the `token_usage` totals leave the owner card path. `lib/business-os/usage/usageSummary.ts` stays for the admin LLM usage report's Check 5, whose claim to compute "exactly what the card shows" ends here (SQ-23). `breakdown`, `daily`, `calls` and `range` go unless another consumer exists (SQ-28).
6. **Remove the temporary R-7 rule — required** (AC-36, KI-17; the T6c list in `docs/workplans/BUSINESS_OS_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_SLICE_5_WORKPLAN.md`): delete `withCustomerDisplay`, `SHOWN_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` and `INCLUDED_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` and their call sites (`buildCustomerPlanView`'s included list, `buildUpgrade`'s next-plan side, `describePlanOffer` in `planOfferView.ts`); delete `creditsShownWithoutAmount.temporary.test.ts` and the TEMPORARY test in `inviteOffer.test.ts`; restore every assertion marked "TEMPORARY (R-7 option C)" to the value its marker names — in `customerPlanView.test.ts` (trial `changes`, Essentials `improves`, the included-list summary, the rank threshold back to 2) and in `PlanSection.render.test.tsx` (back to `renderFor('trial')`, the synthetic cross-unit fixture removed). SA may keep the `email.volume` positive control, which the slice 5 code review called the better control anyway. Update the "Credits (included)" callout in `BUSINESS_OS_ENTITLEMENTS.md` and the pricing doc's §1 note and §8 row. Exit check: a grep for `withCustomerDisplay`, `SHOWN_WITHOUT_AMOUNT`, `INCLUDED_WITHOUT_AMOUNT` and `TEMPORARY (R-7` returns nothing.
7. **Optional — the invite page in the invitee's language** (slice 5 QA-2, OI-10): pass the invite's locale through `describePlanOffer` to `describePlanCapabilities`; the same root makes "per month" / "in total" English on Settings in he / es. BA recommends doing at least the credits line, since slice 6 is what makes it visible to prospects.
8. **Only if the user picks D-g B or C:** a neutral catalog category for `credits.allowance`, its heading key in three languages and the category-order test (SQ-25), under the `business-os-entitlements` skill's rules.
9. **Tests.** Route integration test: happy path, 401, invalid input (CLAUDE.md coverage), plus no plan row, trial total, nothing used, over the allowance (clamped), the split adding up, the payload allow-list, and no way to name another account. Card render tests for every §1 state in en / he / es. A source guard that the card starts no interval or timer (AC-33's "no periodic requests"). A cross-check that the card's "used" for an account and period equals the credits on the Costs & credits tab (slice 4a) for the same account and period. QA records a manual check of the three refresh triggers (AC-33).
10. **Docs.** The entitlements doc and the pricing doc as in item 6; A-18 gains a "superseded by slice 6" note when it ships.

**Guardrails added by this scoping** (on top of the slice 6 table's): no service-role read added to an owner route unless SA rules it (SQ-21); no new ledger column or migration for the split unless SA rules it (SQ-22); no warning, pause or upgrade wording on the card (slices 8, 10); no grant logic (slice 11).

##### 4. Size and split

| Work | Estimate | Sub-slice |
|---|---|---|
| Route: period, allowance, used, split, remaining; repository read; auth / Zod / Pino; integration tests | 1 d | 6a |
| Card: content, states, trial variant, D-a / D-b / D-c wording in en / he / es, refresh icon and tab return (triggers b, c), render tests | 1 d | 6a |
| Retire the rate keys and the `token_usage` path from the owner card; relabel Check 5 (SQ-23) | 0.25 d | 6a |
| Remove the R-7 rule, restore the T6c assertions, docs | 0.5 d | 6a |
| Trigger (a): the signal (SQ-24) and the owner AI surfaces that raise it; QA manual check of AC-33 | 0.5–1 d | 6b |
| Optional: invite page in the invitee's language (QA-2) | 0.5 d | 6b |
| Optional: the neutral heading (D-g) | 0.25 d | 6b |
| **Total** | **≈ 3.5–4.5 d** | |

That is above the "few days per slice" comfort line, so BA proposes a split:

- **6a — The card reads credits** (≈ 2.75 days): items 1–6, 9 and 10, with refresh triggers (b) and (c). It is releasable on its own and carries the required R-7 removal, so "slice 6 is not done until the rule is removed" is met by 6a.
- **6b — The card stays current after the owner's own action** (≈ 0.5–1.75 days): trigger (a) and the rest of AC-33, plus the optional invite localisation and the optional heading.

Both follow D-d: they reach owners together with slice 7. ACs: 6a — AC-18, AC-21, AC-35 (card half), AC-36 (after half), AC-37; 6b — AC-33.

### Slice 7 — Credit diary and typical range

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | The owner can see every AI action and what it cost, and — once there are enough examples — a typical range from their own history. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | A list such as "Today 09:14 — Chat — Answered a question — you — 4 credits" and "Last night — Insights — automatic — 12 credits", with failed actions marked; and, where there is enough history, "website generations have cost you 25–35 credits". |
| **Scope (in)** | FR-26 (reads **only** the charge table; area and label from the slice 1 list; paged, newest first, current period by default), FR-27 (own-history range), FR-6's display rule with the footnote "lines are rounded; the total is exact" (SQ-8), failed actions marked (FR-8). **Every service, neutral wording (BD-15, user 2026-09-29):** the diary shows the rows of **every** `service` in the charge table — only `ai` in this layer — so that a later service's actions (e.g. the 30 emails of Scenario 4) appear without a diary change; a row whose service declares no label is shown under a generic label. The diary's heading and wording are neutral, never "AI credits"; the **final wording is decided in this slice**, consistent with slice 6. How a multi-action group (one grouping id, many action ids) is displayed — one line per action or one collapsible line per group — is a slice 7 display choice. **Effective service (SA N-10 / N-11, 2026-09-29):** each diary line's service is its **effective service** — the row's own `service`, else the service of the charge it adjusts — so an adjustment is shown and labelled under the same service as the charge it corrects; the area and the diary label are looked up by **(effective service, action type)**, never by action type alone. |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Never shows prompts, owner text, model output, error messages, tokens or dollars. No account id parameter. Never reads `audit_trail` or `token_usage`. The range is never a single number and never presented as a price; it is hidden when there are too few examples. *(Added 2026-09-29:)* Does not filter to `service = 'ai'`; never says "AI credits"; builds no non-AI diary label. *(Added 2026-09-29, N-10 / N-11:)* No grouping or labelling by the raw `service` column (adjustments would fall out or land under a blank service); no label lookup by action type alone. |
| **Depends on** | Slices 3 and 6. |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-6 (display), FR-8 (display), FR-26, FR-27, FR-28, **FR-40d, f**. AC-5 (display), AC-19, AC-20, **AC-35 (diary half)**. Also SA-S5, **BD-15**, **N-10**, **N-11**. |

### Slice 8 — Warnings at 80% and 100%, and the setup pre-warning

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Owners are warned before they run out, and before a setup action that could use up the rest of a trial. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Once warnings are switched on: a notice at 80% and at 100%, each once per period; and, before a setup regeneration on a trial, "this usually costs about X of your remaining Y credits — continue?", with a way to cancel. **In `shadow` the warnings are recorded and logged, not sent** — Barak can see who would have been warned. |
| **Scope (in)** | FR-23: configurable thresholds (default 80 / 100), once per threshold per period, the setup pre-warning. The estimate for "would exhaust" is the action type's measured p90 from slice 4, or the owner's own history once there is enough (SA-S4). Delivery through the existing in-app surface. |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Refuses nothing. The estimate is never the charge. No new delivery channel (email, SMS). Nothing sent to the owner's clients. Whether warnings are sent before enforcement (SQ-11 condition 3 allows it after slice 5) is a switch-on choice, not a build choice. |
| **Depends on** | Slices 3, 4 (the p90 figures) and 5 (real allowances to measure against). |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-23. AC-17. Also SA-S4, SQ-11 condition 3. |

### Slice 9 — Check the balance before every AI action

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Before every Business OS AI action, ask one question in one place: "does this account have anything left?" — and behave sensibly when the answer cannot be read. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Nothing visible in `shadow`. The check runs, its answer is logged, and nothing is refused. Once enforcement is on, this is what decides whether the owner's assistant may run. |
| **Scope (in)** | The real `balance.ts` (FR-17): plan allowance first, then grants (none exist until slice 11), consumption read fresh from the totals row (SQ-5). The pre-check wired in **one** place, `runAiAction`, before the action runs, with the audience from the slice 1 list (SA-B3). chat-v4 restructured to set the account before the action starts. The gate registered in `ENFORCEMENT_POINTS`. **Outage behaviour per BQ-1 option C** (FR-21, SA-B2): when the balance cannot be read, owner AI keeps working unless the account was last seen at zero, then `entitlement_unavailable`; the comment and catch block in `EntitlementService` updated in the same change. The pre-check asks only "is anything left?" — no reservation (SQ-4). **Folded in from the SA follow-up (2026-09-28):** option C needs a **"last seen at zero"** fact that is readable **when the balance is not** — the totals row is unreadable in exactly that case. The workplan must say **where it is kept** (for example, per-instance memory from the last successful read), and a **cold instance with no memory keeps working** (treated as "last seen with credit left") — otherwise option C silently becomes option A or B. *(Annotation 2026-10-02, from slice 11 scoping: slice 9 counts the lot balance of `business_os_credit_lots` (built by 11a) after the plan allowance, soonest-expiring first, then lots with no expiry, oldest first among equals (FR-17, boost FR-31). `granted` per R-5 (b) and S11-SQ-4. Who writes draws, and when, is S11-SQ-3. AC-13's grant half is delivered here.)* |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Refuses nothing in `shadow`, and **cannot throw** there (WC-21). No reservation. No second pre-check anywhere else. No change to the chat daily budget or the image cap. Does not use `account_frozen`. Never an upgrade prompt on an outage. A missing "last seen" fact never refuses an owner. |
| **Depends on** | Slices 1 and 3. |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-5 (the pre-check estimate), FR-17, FR-18 (period reset applied), FR-21, FR-38. AC-13 (plan half), AC-15, AC-27. Also SA-B2, SA-B3, BQ-1, OI-5. |

### Slice 10 — Pause the owner's assistant, keep client work on templates

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Define and build what happens at zero: the owner's own AI pauses with a clear explanation; everything that touches the owner's clients keeps working on template text. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Nothing in `shadow`. Once enforcement is on, at zero: "Your assistant is paused until the 14th, or until you upgrade" — while their clients still get lead replies and reminders. A turn stopped by the daily chat limit says so, and is never confused with the monthly allowance. |
| **Scope (in)** | FR-19: pause owner-facing actions, degrade client-facing ones, by audience. FR-20: **route "balance exhausted" down the existing fallback path** for `lead_reply_recommendation` — the same path the area-switched-off path already uses (`pickFallbackCandidate`, a fixed rule with no AI call) — so a lead reply goes out even with no AI (C-1). FR-22: distinct, correctly named refusals for the daily chat budget and the period allowance. The pause message as a normal assistant reply that keeps the conversation usable. *(Superseded wording, corrected by C-1: "FR-20: **build the missing template fallback** for `lead_reply_recommendation` (OI-1) so a lead reply goes out even with no AI.")* |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Inert in `shadow`. No client-facing automation may ever be stopped. No change to how the chat daily budget or image cap count. No enforcement switch-on. No change to the pre-check itself (slice 9). No new lead-reply fallback and no change to `pickFallbackCandidate`'s rule — only a new reason to take the existing path. |
| **Depends on** | Slice 9. |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-19, FR-20, FR-22. AC-14, AC-16. Also OI-1 (closed by C-1), BD-5, C-1. |

### Slice 11 — Admin grant / reduce credits and per-account view

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | An admin can give an account extra credits, take them back, and see any account's position — without SQL. |
| **What the owner / user gets** | Barak or an admin can give a design partner or an unhappy customer "+200 credits, reason: onboarding help, expires 31 Dec", and reduce or end it later. The owner sees the extra credits in their remaining balance, used only after the plan's own credits. An admin can open one account and see allowance, used, granted, remaining and which layer decided the allowance. |
| **Scope (in)** | FR-30: new grant / reduce / end operations on the existing admin operations path — `requireAdmin` first, Zod-validated, audited with actor, target, before/after and reason. A grants table carrying actor, reason, `created_at` and `expires_at` (SA-S8). Grants counted in `balance.ts`, soonest-expiring first (FR-17). FR-31: the per-account position view. *(Annotation 2026-10-02, BA slice 11 scoping: "A grants table carrying actor, reason, `created_at` and `expires_at` (SA-S8)" is **superseded** by the boost SA ruling T-2 / R-5 (a): admin grants are rows of `source = 'admin_grant'` in the shared append-only `business_os_credit_lots` table, written only through `business_os_record_credit_lot(...)`, which **slice 11 creates** (11a); reduce / end is an append-only reversal row against a lot. SA-S8's standards apply to that table. "Grants counted in `balance.ts`, soonest-expiring first (FR-17)" moves to **slice 9**, where `balance.ts` becomes real. Split into 11a–11d: see [Slice 11 scoping](#slice-11-scoping-ba-2026-10-02).)* |
| **Guardrails (out / must not touch)** | Never available to a user on their own account. No purchase or payment (S-4b). No Pilot-Credit table. Never `profiles.role`. Grants never rewrite charge rows. *(Annotation 2026-10-02: lots and reversals never touch `business_os_credit_totals` or `business_os_record_credit_charge` (T-2); no draw / consumption rows in slice 11; the owner never sees a combined total (R-5 (c)).)* |
| **Depends on** | Slices 3 and 9. *(Annotation 2026-10-02: 11a–11c depend on slice 3 only — they do not need slice 9, because counting grants in `balance.ts` moved to slice 9. Slice 9 now depends on 11a for the lots table it counts. 11d may wait for slice 9 or boost slice 5, per S11-D-1.)* |
| **FRs / ACs** | FR-17 (grant half), FR-30, FR-31. AC-13 (grant half), AC-22, AC-23 (view half). Also A-14, D-7. *(Annotation 2026-10-02: plus S11-AC-1 to S11-AC-10; AC-13's grant half moves to slice 9 with the consumption order. Also boost T-2, R-5, RV-4, RV-6.)* |
