#### Formal acknowledgement of boost T-2 / R-5 (for the credit deduction requirement)

**SA, on behalf of the credit deduction requirement, acknowledges and adopts boost T-2 and R-5 (a)–(c) in full, as corrected below for the change of ownership.** This is the acknowledgement boost RV-6 and the boost slice 2 gate are waiting for.

1. **One table for every credit added.** Admin grants and boost purchases are rows of one append-only table, `business_os_credit_lots`, distinguished by `source` (`'admin_grant'`, `'boost_purchase'`). Slice 11 builds no grants table; SA-S8's standards apply to the lots table.
2. **One write function.** Lots are written only through `business_os_record_credit_lot(...)`, `SECURITY INVOKER`, EXECUTE to service_role only, idempotent on a unique `idempotency_key`. Boost's `business_os_credit_boost_purchase` (T-6) calls it inside its own transaction.
3. **Movement out of a lot is append-only.** Reduce and end are rows in `business_os_credit_lot_draws`; a lot row is never updated or deleted, and no mutable "remaining" column exists. Remaining is always rebuilt from the rows.
4. **Lots never touch the charge path.** No lot or draw writes `business_os_credit_charges`, `business_os_credit_totals` or calls `business_os_record_credit_charge`; period resets cannot affect a lot; C7 is unchanged by construction and is re-run to prove it.
5. **Two figures for the owner** (R-5 (c)): plan left and extra credits are never combined in anything shown to an owner, nor carried as a combined figure in the owner payload (S11-SQ-10).
6. **`granted` / lot balance** (R-5 (b)) as ruled in S11-SQ-4: "at the start of the period" qualifies the **draws** subtracted, not the lots counted.
7. **Consumption order is binding on slice 9:** plan allowance first, then lots soonest-expiring first, then lots with no expiry, oldest first among equals (FR-17, boost FR-31).

**Changed by the ordering flip (slice 11 now creates the table):** (a) the `source` CHECK admits both values from day one — boost slice 2 needs no CHECK change; (b) the boost-purchase row shape is fixed now (below), so boost adds **no column** to the lots table; (c) **boost slice 2's migration (block 20261030–34, boost R-4) creates only its purchase and cap-override tables and functions** and must not alter `business_os_credit_lots` or `business_os_credit_lot_draws` — including no FK added *from* the lots table (the link is `business_os_boost_purchases.lot_id UNIQUE REFERENCES business_os_credit_lots(id)`, owned by boost); (d) `BusinessOsCreditLotRepository` is created by 11a and extended (not recreated) by boost slice 2 for its purchase-side needs. **Boost slice 2 may build only after 20261017 is applied to PROD.** BA records (c) and (d) in the boost requirement (S11-C-1).

#### Corrections to the scoping

- **S11-CR-1 (pre-checks).** Add the platform-account refusal (F11-12): 409 `platform_account` before the tenant check, for both credit ops and the view route.
- **S11-CR-2 (replay and audit).** S11-AC-2 ("no second audit entry") cannot hold through the route as built (F11-3). The outcome gains a way to say "replayed, do not audit" (S11-SQ-5 ruling).
- **S11-CR-3 (S11-AC-6, "combined remaining").** Slice 11 shows **no combined remaining**, to admins or owners. With no draws, `allowance + lots − used` and "plan left + lots" disagree whenever an account has used more than its allowance in shadow (S11-KI-2), and which one is right is exactly slice 9's draw design. The admin view shows plan left and extra credits as two figures, plus an "over the plan this period" figure (`max(0, used − allowance)`) when it is above 0. The combined figure arrives with slice 9.
- **S11-CR-4 (row locking).** The reversal function cannot lock the lot row (F11-7); it takes a per-account advisory lock. The same key is binding on slice 9 / 10's consumption writer.

#### S11-SQ rulings

| SQ | Ruling (all TECHNICAL) |
|---|---|
| **S11-SQ-1** — lots table, T-2 / R-5 | **(a)** Acknowledged above. **(b)** Both sources from day one. **(c)** Per-source shape CHECKs, as specified in "The tables" below. **(d)** Typed actor: `actor_kind` (`'admin'` / `'stripe_webhook'`) plus `actor_admin_id uuid` with **no FK** (F11-5, RC-9), required for `admin`, NULL for `stripe_webhook`. **(e)** Admin key = `'admin_grant:' ∥ requestId`, `requestId` a UUID in the body (`z.string().uuid()`), minted by the admin UI once per form opening and kept across retries, renewed only after a success. **On a key that already exists**, the function returns the existing lot only if it belongs to the same account, the same source and the same amount; otherwise it raises, and the executor answers 409 `idempotency_key_conflict` — a reused key never returns another account's lot (tenant isolation) |
| **S11-SQ-2** — reversal table | **One table, `business_os_credit_lot_draws`**, as the BA suggests, **but its `kind` CHECK admits `'reversal'` only.** SA overrules the lean to add `'consumption'` now: a consumption row needs columns no one has designed — at least `period_start`, because R-5 (b) subtracts draws "recorded in earlier periods", and probably a link to the charge or the period total it covers. Admitting the value now would either force those columns before a writer exists (T-2's caution) or let the first consumption writer add rows that cannot answer R-5 (b). Slice 9 / 10 adds `'consumption'`, its shape CHECK and its columns in one migration (20261019 is held for it). A CHECK swap on a small append-only table is cheap |
| **S11-SQ-3** — consumption draws | **Confirmed: slice 11 writes none; no backfill** (S11-KI-2 stands). Slice 9 / 10 decides the writer, constrained by: never inside `business_os_record_credit_charge` (T-2); same per-account advisory lock as reversals (S11-CR-4); append-only; idempotent on its own key. SA's recommendation for slice 9 is R-5's **second branch** — an explicit "allowance first, then lots" computation over draws — because the closed formula mis-states a lot that expires mid-period after part of it was used this period (its consumed credits would fall back onto the plan figure) |
| **S11-SQ-4** — what `granted` means | **The BA reading is right; SA rules it as the definition.** One pure function (`lib/business-os/credits/creditLots.ts`, built in 11a): per lot, `remaining = credits_granted − Σ reversals − Σ consumption draws` (consumption 0 until slice 9), `expired = expires_at IS NOT NULL AND expires_at ≤ at`; **extra credits at `at`** = Σ `remaining` over lots not expired at `at`, **including lots created during the current period**, counted from their `created_at`. A lot stops counting at its expiry instant (the same `≤` comparison as the reversal function, pinned by a test). **R-5 (b)'s "at the start of the period" applies to the draws subtracted**, not to the lots counted: a grant on the 20th counts on the 20th. In slice 11 that is simply granted − reversals on unexpired lots. The admin view (11c), the owner figure (11d) and slice 9 all call this one function, so they read one number. The enforcement-side formula (how `used` and lots combine) is slice 9's, under S11-SQ-3 |
| **S11-SQ-5** — same union or sibling | **Same route, same union**, as the BA suggests (FR-30). The two variants `grant_credits` and `reduce_credit_lot` (no third `end_*` op: `amount: 'rest'` ends a lot) are **defined in** a new `lib/business-os/credits/creditAdminOps.ts` (schemas, pre-checks, executor) and composed into `adminOpSchema` and dispatched from `executeAdminOp`. The credit file **imports nothing from the entitlements module**: it declares its own context and outcome types, and `adminOps.ts` adapts them — so the dependency points one way and no new non-gate importer is created. `AdminOpOutcome` gains: status `403`; an optional `audit` override (`entityType: 'business_os_credit_lot'`, `entityId` = lot id, `changes` = before / after figures — see S11-SQ-11) that the route uses instead of the plan-row defaults; an optional `replayed: true` on which the route **writes no audit entry** and logs at `info` (S11-CR-2); and an optional `invalidatesEntitlements: false` so credit ops skip the entitlement cache invalidation (`:230`) they do not need. The 7 existing ops' outcomes and audit rows are unchanged — a route test pins one existing op's audit call byte-for-byte |
| **S11-SQ-6** — self-account guard | In `executeAdminOp`, **first, before any read**: `resolveAccountId(ctx.adminId) === ctx.accountId` → 403 `own_account`, for the ops listed in one exported set. **11b applies it to the two credit ops** (FR-30, AC-22 — in scope). Extending the set to the 7 existing ops is a one-line change to that set, but it changes what an admin can do to their own account today, including on the account they test with; that is a policy choice, not a technical one, so it goes to the user as **S11-BQ-1**. If the user says yes, it lands in 11b (≈ +0.25 d, tests per op); if no, S11-KI-4 stays open with the user's answer recorded. The guard compares logins (S11-KI-3 stands) |
| **S11-SQ-7** — tenant isolation | **As the BA suggests, plus:** the account comes only from the URL path (`resolveAccountId`), never the body (the variants carry no account id; `.strict()` refuses one); platform account refused (S11-CR-1); `isBusinessOsTenant` and the plan-row check as today; a reduction reads the lot **within the account** through the repository (404 `lot_not_found`, F11-4 pattern), and the reversal function re-checks `lot.user_id = p_user_id` under the lock and answers `lot_not_found` for a foreign lot exactly as for a missing one; the record function takes `p_user_id` from the executor, never from caller data; the idempotency rule of S11-SQ-1 (e). Required tests (skill Step 7): a lot id of another account → 404 and **the reversal function is never called**; an injected `accountId` / `userId` in the body → 400 |
| **S11-SQ-8** — migration number | **20261017**, one migration: both tables, both functions, grants, RLS. **20261018** held for a slice 11 follow-up (for example an owner-note column if S11-D-4 C is chosen after 11a is applied). **20261019** held for slice 9 / 10's consumption draws. Applying 20261017 before 20261016 (deferred slice 4c) is fine — they are independent — and the runbook says so |
| **S11-SQ-9** — the admin view's figures | **A new `GET /api/admin/business-os/credits/accounts/[accountId]`**, beside the credits report and leak check, as the BA suggests: `requireAdmin` first, Zod uuid on the path, no other input (a test that it ignores the query), 409 `platform_account`, 404 tenant, `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`, Pino child with `correlationId`, the CLAUDE.md error format. **Reuse, not copy:** extract the body of `readOwnerCreditUsage` into `readCreditPosition(accountId, deps, log)` in the same module; `readOwnerCreditUsage(userId, …)` stays a thin wrapper that resolves the session account (unchanged behaviour; its tests stay green unedited). The admin route builds its deps in a new `adminCreditPositionDeps.ts` that constructs `BusinessOsCreditOwnerReadRepository` **on the service-role client, explicitly**, documented in code as the intentional RLS bypass of an admin-only read (account from the path after `requireAdmin` and the tenant check); that repository selects owner-safe columns only, so no cost column can reach the admin payload by this path. The allowance **and its deciding layer** come from a small read helper exported from inside the entitlements module (keeping the `'credits.allowance'` literal inside the module, as the slice 6 ruling did); the route is registered in `KNOWN_NON_GATE_IMPORTERS`. Lots and draws through `BusinessOsCreditLotRepository`; extra credits through `creditLots.ts`; no combined remaining (S11-CR-3). Payload: credits only, no USD, no owner text |
| **S11-SQ-10** — owner payload | **The `granted` key is replaced by `extraCredits`** (exact, 6 dp; rounding display-only through `creditDisplay.ts`), and **`remaining` stays plan-only** (`computeCreditBalance` called with `granted: 0`). Filling `granted` instead would turn the payload's `remaining` into a combined figure that no screen shows but any future reader could (F11-9, F11-10) — against R-5 (c). The owner reads lots and draws through **new methods on `BusinessOsCreditOwnerReadRepository`** (owner RLS client, owner-granted columns only, subset-tested against the new GRANT lines, parsed from 20261017). The allow-list pin is changed deliberately in 11d (boost RV-5); under S11-D-1 A boost slice 5 then adds **no** key. **`computeCreditBalance` is not changed in slice 11** (null without an allowance stays); with two separate figures an account with lots and no allowance simply shows the extra figure, so S11-KI-5 is moot for display and passes to slice 9 for enforcement |
| **S11-SQ-11** — audit | **`BOS_CREDIT_LOT_GRANTED` and `BOS_CREDIT_LOT_REDUCED`** in `AUDIT_EVENTS`, swept by `adminOps.test.ts` (extend the sweep to `creditAdminOps.ts`); severity `warning`; `entityType 'business_os_credit_lot'`, `entityId` the lot id, `userId` the account, `actorId` the admin. `changes`: before / after the account's extra credits and, for a reduction, the lot's remaining before / after **as returned by the reversal function under its lock** (authoritative); details: lot id, source, credits, expiry, idempotency key, correlation id, reason (as every op does) — never owner text. Flushed before the response (WC-7, already in the route). A replay writes no entry (S11-CR-2) |
| **S11-SQ-12** — registries | **As the BA suggests, for both tables:** `businessOwnedTables.ts` (account-keyed to `auth.users`), `purge/descriptors.ts` `never` (financial record), `accountDeletionPolicy.ts` `minimise` (`user_id ON DELETE SET NULL`, no UPDATE grant needed, as charges); a business Reset cannot reach them. The registry guard tests pass in 11a |
| **S11-SQ-13** — typo ceiling | **One exported constant** in `lib/business-os/credits/` read by the Zod schema (`.int().positive().max(CEILING)`), a test pinning it; **no table CHECK** (policy, not data — agreed). Admin amounts are **whole credits** (integers) for both ops; `'rest'` covers a fractional remainder once draws exist. If the user picks S11-D-5 A, the constant still exists as an absolute sanity ceiling of 10,000,000, so a pasted id or a runaway number is refused |
| **S11-SQ-14** — expiry | **As the BA suggests:** the form sends an ISO instant; the UI converts a picked date to 23:59:59.999 UTC that day and says "ends 23:59 UTC on …" beside the field; the server refuses an expiry at or before `now` (400 `expires_at_in_past`), and the table has `CHECK (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > created_at)` as the backstop. The admin view shows expiry in UTC, labelled (an admin surface; the business timezone rule governs client-facing hours) |
| **S11-SQ-15** — entitlements registration | **11b: no new importer** if built as ruled (the credit file imports nothing from the module; `adminOps.ts` is inside it). **11c:** the view route (and `adminCreditPositionDeps.ts` only if it imports from the module) registered as non-gate importers with exact symbols; the new read helper lives inside the module. **11d:** `ownerCreditUsage.ts`'s existing entry updated only if its symbols change. `BusinessOsPanel.tsx` imports nothing from the module. `npm run test:bos-entitlements` green on each sub-slice's final diff |
| **S11-SQ-16** — admin register and guard | The new GET has `requireAdmin` as its first statement; a row in the register of `docs/admin/ADMIN_IDENTIFICATION_AND_ACCESS.md` with the census re-measured and the `Admin authz surface guard` caps moved by exactly one handler in the same PR (equality caps). 11b adds **no** handler. The existing POST is not in `adminGate.writes`; adding it is not required by slice 11 |

#### The tables (11a, migration 20261017) — to the level the workplan needs

**`public.business_os_credit_lots`**

| Column | Type | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | `uuid` PK | `DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` |
| `user_id` | `uuid` NULL | FK `auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` (minimise); the function refuses NULL |
| `source` | `text NOT NULL` | `CHECK (source IN ('admin_grant', 'boost_purchase'))` |
| `credits_granted` | `numeric(18,6) NOT NULL` | `> 0`, `<> 'NaN'` |
| `credits_base` | `numeric(18,6) NOT NULL` | `>= 0` |
| `credits_bonus` | `numeric(18,6) NOT NULL` | `>= 0`; `CHECK (credits_base + credits_bonus = credits_granted)` |
| `credit_value_version` | `integer NOT NULL` | `>= 0` (the credit value in force when the lot was written) |
| `expires_at` | `timestamptz` NULL | NULL = no end; `CHECK (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > created_at)` |
| `idempotency_key` | `text NOT NULL` | `UNIQUE`; 1–200 characters |
| `source_ref` | `uuid` NULL | boost: the purchase id; **no FK** (the purchase table is boost's, and boost may not alter this table) |
| `actor_kind` | `text NOT NULL` | `CHECK (actor_kind IN ('admin', 'stripe_webhook'))` |
| `actor_admin_id` | `uuid` NULL | **no FK** (F11-5) |
| `reason` | `text` NULL | trimmed length 3–500 when present |
| `created_at` | `timestamptz NOT NULL` | `DEFAULT now()` |

Shape CHECKs (named, like the charges' `kind` shapes): **`admin_grant`** ⇒ `actor_kind = 'admin' AND actor_admin_id IS NOT NULL AND reason IS NOT NULL AND source_ref IS NULL AND credits_bonus = 0 AND left(idempotency_key, 12) = 'admin_grant:'`; **`boost_purchase`** ⇒ `actor_kind = 'stripe_webhook' AND actor_admin_id IS NULL AND reason IS NULL AND source_ref IS NOT NULL AND left(idempotency_key, 6) = 'boost:'` (T-5's key). `expires_at` is **not** constrained for boost (v1 writes NULL; a later boost expiry needs no change here). If the user picks **S11-D-4 C**, add `owner_note text NULL` (trimmed 3–200, `admin_grant` only) **in 20261017** — hence S11-C-1. Indexes: `(user_id, created_at)`; the unique key.

**`public.business_os_credit_lot_draws`**

| Column | Type | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | `uuid` PK | `DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()` |
| `lot_id` | `uuid NOT NULL` | FK `business_os_credit_lots(id)` (lots are never deleted) |
| `user_id` | `uuid` NULL | FK `auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL`; equals the lot's (function-enforced; checker-verified) |
| `kind` | `text NOT NULL` | `CHECK (kind IN ('reversal'))` — slice 9 / 10 adds `'consumption'` (S11-SQ-2) |
| `credits` | `numeric(18,6) NOT NULL` | `> 0`, `<> 'NaN'` |
| `reason` | `text` NULL | trimmed 3–500 when present |
| `actor_admin_id` | `uuid` NULL | no FK |
| `idempotency_key` | `text NOT NULL` | `UNIQUE`; 1–200 characters |
| `created_at` | `timestamptz NOT NULL` | `DEFAULT now()` |

Shape CHECK: **`reversal`** ⇒ `reason IS NOT NULL AND actor_admin_id IS NOT NULL AND left(idempotency_key, 15) = 'admin_reversal:'`. Indexes: `(lot_id)`, `(user_id, created_at)`, the unique key.

**Grants and RLS (both tables).** RLS on; one policy each, `FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING ((SELECT auth.uid()) = user_id)`. `REVOKE ALL` from PUBLIC, anon, authenticated and service_role, then: `authenticated` column SELECT — lots `(id, user_id, source, credits_granted, credits_base, credits_bonus, expires_at, created_at)`; draws `(id, lot_id, user_id, kind, credits, created_at)` (reason, actors, keys, `source_ref` and `credit_value_version` hidden; a column added later is hidden by default). `service_role`: `SELECT, INSERT` only. **No UPDATE, DELETE, TRUNCATE, REFERENCES or TRIGGER to any of the four roles.** No triggers on either table.

**`business_os_record_credit_lot(p_user_id uuid, p_source text, p_credits_base numeric, p_credits_bonus numeric, p_credit_value_version integer, p_expires_at timestamptz, p_idempotency_key text, p_source_ref uuid, p_actor_kind text, p_actor_admin_id uuid, p_reason text) RETURNS TABLE (out_recorded boolean, out_lot_id uuid)`** — `VOLATILE`, `SECURITY INVOKER`, `SET search_path = ''`. Refuses a NULL user or key (`22004`); rounds amounts to 6 dp; `credits_granted = base + bonus`; `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (idempotency_key) DO NOTHING RETURNING id`; on conflict reads the existing row and returns `(false, id)` only when `user_id`, `source` and `credits_granted` match, else raises (`23505`, message "idempotency key reused for a different lot"). CHECK violations surface as `23514` — the executor's pre-checks make them unreachable from the admin path (M-3: a constraint is the backstop, not the message).

**`business_os_reverse_credit_lot(p_user_id uuid, p_lot_id uuid, p_credits numeric, p_idempotency_key text, p_actor_admin_id uuid, p_reason text) RETURNS TABLE (out_status text, out_draw_id uuid, out_credits numeric, out_remaining_before numeric, out_remaining_after numeric)`** — same attributes. `p_credits` NULL means "the rest". Steps, in one transaction: (1) `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('business_os_credit_lots:' ∥ p_user_id, 0))` — **the per-account key slice 9 / 10 must reuse**; (2) a draw with this key exists → `already_recorded` with its figures if it is on the same lot and account, else raise as above; (3) lot not found **or** not this account's → `lot_not_found`; (4) `expires_at ≤ now()` → `lot_expired`; (5) remaining = granted − Σ draws; ≤ 0 → `nothing_left`; (6) `p_credits` ≤ 0 → raise; > remaining → `exceeds_remaining` (S11-D-6 A); (7) insert the `reversal` row, return `recorded`. Every non-recorded status writes nothing. EXECUTE on both functions: service_role only (REVOKE from PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role first, as `:267-285`).

**Repository and pure code (11a).** `lib/repositories/BusinessOsCreditLotRepository.ts` (`new-repository` skill: service-role default with the documented reason, client injectable for tests, `RepositoryResult`, Pino, never throws): `recordLot(input)` and `reverseLot(input)` via `.rpc()` only (a source test: no `.insert(` / `.update(` / `.delete(` / `.upsert(`); `listLotsWithDraws(accountId)` with `.eq('user_id', accountId)` on both reads (admin columns, explicit lists, never `*`), numeric strings parsed to finite numbers or an error, never 0 (the `figure()` rule); `findLotForAccount(lotId, accountId)`. `lib/business-os/credits/creditLots.ts` (S11-SQ-4), pure, unit-tested: fractional amounts, a lot created mid-period, expiry exactly at `at`, a fully reversed lot, an empty list. Entitlements doc: a "Credits added: lots" paragraph under § Metering. Nothing wired.

#### The runbook (11a) — slice 3 §6.4.1 style

Four files, all pasteable whole, **no `--` comments**, short standalone statements, no single-letter aliases (F11-16): the migration, `scripts/check-bos-credit-lots-migration.sql`, `scripts/probe-bos-credit-lots-migration.sql`, `supabase/SQL Scripts/20261017_business_os_credit_lots_rollback.sql`. Steps the user runs, in order: (1) **pre-check** `to_regclass` / `to_regprocedure` on both tables and both exact function signatures → all NULL, else stop; (2) apply; (3) record `now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC'`; (4) the new checker → `VERDICT PASS`; (5) **the existing 20261015 checker → `VERDICT PASS`, C7 PASS** (the charge path untouched); (6) own user id; (7) the probe in a new tab → `PROBE PASS … rolls everything back`; (8) both checkers again → unchanged, L-INFO counts still 0 lots, 0 draws; (9) paste everything into the workplan. The three `PROBE SKIPPED` answers and the destructive-statement warning are explained exactly as §6.4.1 does.

**Checker rows** (read-only session, one grid, row 0 the verdict):

| Row | Check |
|---|---|
| L1 | Both tables exist with RLS on; exactly one policy each, SELECT, to `authenticated`, `auth.uid() = user_id` |
| L2 | `authenticated` holds **exactly** the listed column SELECTs (compared both ways) and no table-level privilege; PUBLIC and anon hold nothing |
| L3 | `service_role` holds exactly `SELECT, INSERT` on both; no role other than the owner holds UPDATE, DELETE, TRUNCATE, REFERENCES or TRIGGER |
| L4 | Every named CHECK, both UNIQUE keys and the three FKs present (`user_id` → `auth.users` with `SET NULL`, `lot_id` → lots); no FK on `actor_admin_id` or `source_ref`; no triggers |
| L5 | Both functions exist with the exact signatures, `SECURITY INVOKER`, `search_path` pinned empty |
| L6 | EXECUTE on both: service_role only; no PUBLIC / anon / authenticated entry |
| L7 | **Lot-balance rebuild parity:** (a) no lot has `credits_granted − Σ draws < 0`; (b) every draw's `user_id` equals its lot's (or both NULL); (c) no draw is older than its lot; (d) Σ per account of per-lot remaining equals Σ over the account's draws subtracted from its lots computed the other way (a GROUP BY account versus a GROUP BY lot, compared); (e) every idempotency key's prefix matches its source / kind |
| L8 | **Charge path byte-identical:** `md5(prosrc)` of `business_os_record_credit_charge` and `business_os_credit_period_start` equal constants that a Jest test computes from the bodies in `20261015` (so a drift between file and checker fails in Jest, not on PROD); column lists of charges and totals unchanged |
| L9 | INFO: lots by source, draws by kind, accounts with lots, unexpired remaining total, first `created_at` (UTC) |
| L10 | INFO: checked at UTC |

**Probe** (one `DO` block, user pastes their own id, `SET LOCAL ROLE service_role` before every write and `authenticated` with JWT claims for the RLS steps, always ends in `RAISE EXCEPTION`): P00 baseline counts (INFO); P01 admin grant recorded; P02 same key → not recorded, same id, count unchanged; P03 same key, different amount → raises; P04 unknown source refused; P05 admin grant with no reason, with a `source_ref`, with a bonus, with a wrong key prefix → each refused; P06 credits 0, negative, `NaN` refused; P07 a boost-shaped lot accepted, and refused with an admin actor; P08 partial reversal recorded with before / after; P09 replay → `already_recorded`, no row; P10 more than left → `exceeds_remaining`, no row; P11 `NULL` credits ("the rest") → remaining 0; P12 again → `nothing_left`; P13 the lot under a stranger's id → `lot_not_found`; P14 as service_role, UPDATE / DELETE / TRUNCATE on both tables refused (`42501`); P15 as the owner, direct INSERT and EXECUTE of both functions refused; own lot and draws readable; `reason` and `idempotency_key` refused (`42501`); P16 a stranger sees none of the rows; P17 charges and totals counts unchanged after every step. Expiry (`lot_expired`) cannot be produced inside one transaction (`now()` is fixed and the CHECK forbids a past expiry); it is covered by the migration tests, and P18 says so as INFO.

**Rollback** refuses (`ROLLBACK REFUSED …`, nothing dropped) when either table holds a row; otherwise drops the functions, then draws, then lots. The Dev re-runs the four files on throwaway PGlite in the runbook's order in the Dev scratchpad, outside the repo (as 3b-i did), and records the result in the workplan. **No agent applies anything to PROD.**

#### The split — ruling

Accepted with one move and re-sized. **The pure lot-balance function and the repository's read methods move into 11a**, so 11c and 11d build on a tested core and the checker's L7 and the code agree on one definition.

| Sub-slice | Scope | Size | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **11a — Credit lots table and the PROD runbook** | Migration 20261017, checker, probe, rollback, PGlite run; `BusinessOsCreditLotRepository`; `creditLots.ts`; registries; entitlements doc paragraph | ≈ 2.5 d | S11-AC-1, S11-AC-8, S11-AC-10; **applied to PROD by the user before 11b merges** |
| **11b — Admin give / take back (API)** | The two ops in `creditAdminOps.ts`, composed into the existing union; self-account guard; pre-checks incl. platform account and payment hold (fail closed); outcome extensions (403, audit override, replay, no cache invalidation); audit keys; D-7 handling; integration tests | ≈ 2 d (+0.25 d if S11-BQ-1 is yes) | S11-AC-2 to S11-AC-5, S11-AC-9 |
| **11c — Admin per-account credit view** | The GET route; `readCreditPosition` extraction; `adminCreditPositionDeps.ts`; the entitlements read helper; the Credits block in `BusinessOsPanel.tsx` with Give / Take back forms (under S11-D-8 B) | ≈ 2 d (≈ 1.5 d under S11-D-8 A) | S11-AC-6 (as corrected by S11-CR-3) |
| **11d — Owner sees extra credits** | `extraCredits` replaces `granted`; owner repository methods; the second figure and its line in en / he / es; the pin updated | ≈ 1 d | S11-AC-7 |
| **Total** | | **≈ 7.5 d, four PRs** | |

11a and 11b stay apart (the migration must be applied by hand before 11b means anything). 11c and 11d are independent of each other and may be built in parallel once 11b (for 11c) or 11a (for 11d) is merged. Small enough: no sub-slice is over 2.5 d.

#### Slice 9 dependency — "allowance first, then lots by soonest expiry"

**Waits for slice 9; no draw rows now.** In `shadow` nothing refuses, so the order changes no outcome today, and writing consumption draws now would mean designing the consumption shape before its only reader (S11-SQ-2, S11-SQ-3). What slice 11 owes slice 9 is fixed here: the table, the per-account lock key, the `creditLots.ts` definition and the binding order.

#### Which S11-D decisions are genuinely business — and SA's constraints

| Decision | Business or technical | SA constraint |
|---|---|---|
| **S11-D-1** owner sees extra credits | **Business** | B is excluded by R-5 (c) (binding). Under A, 11d is built as S11-SQ-10 rules and the boost requirement's "Bought credits" label (T-13) becomes "Extra credits" — a BA edit to the boost requirement, no technical objection. Under C, 11d is dropped and the owner payload is unchanged |
| **S11-D-2** default expiry | **Business** | Under C (recommended) the `expiresAt` key is **required** (ISO or `null`), as `assign_tier` does (A-1); under A it is optional and defaults to `null`; under B the default is a server constant, not a UI default |
| **S11-D-3** trial / held friend | **Business** | Whatever the answer: a lot never changes a plan date (true by construction); a held-friend refusal **reuses `readPaymentHold` and fails closed** (boost R-1), answering 409 `awaiting_payment`; a trial refusal (option B) is decided from the plan row's cohort through the existing executor inputs, never a tier literal |
| **S11-D-4** does the owner see the reason | **Business — needed before 11a** | **B is not available**: the reason is written as internal audit text and is hidden from owners at the column level; showing it would mean granting that column to owners. So the real choice is **A** (fixed label from `source`) or **C** (a separate owner note). **C must be decided before 11a's migration is frozen**, or it costs migration 20261018 later |
| **S11-D-5** largest gift | **Business** | Per gift, whole credits, a server constant (S11-SQ-13). 100,000 is technically fine (numeric(18,6) holds it; ≈ $100 of cost at $0.001). There is no per-day or per-account cap under B: two gifts of 100,000 are possible, and only the audit trail sees them — stated so the user decides knowingly |
| **S11-D-6** take back below zero | **Technical — ruled A by SA; removed from the user's list** | B would need a negative lot balance, which either draws the plan figure down (contradicting the two-figure rule, R-5 (c)) or needs a debt concept nobody has designed. Not a genuine trade-off |
| **S11-D-7** take back bought credits | **Business, not blocking slice 11** | It matters only once boost lots exist. Until the user answers, 11b **refuses a reduction on a `boost_purchase` lot** (409 `paid_credits_locked`), tested with a fixture lot. If the answer is A, the op requires `confirmPaidCredits: true` for such a lot (a one-line change and a test) and the 11c form shows the stronger confirmation |
| **S11-D-8** where the admin acts | **Business** | C is excluded while the Plans page stays read-only by design (until cleanup slice 6). Under B, 11c delivers the allowance / balance half of cleanup 5e, as the BA says; the forms mint the request id (S11-SQ-1 (e)), require a reason, confirm by naming the business, and map every refusal code to a plain sentence (a completeness test like `SUMMARY_ERROR_COPY`'s). Under A, 11c is the read view only and the ops stay API-only |

#### Business question for the user (beyond S11-D-1 to S11-D-8)

- **S11-BQ-1 — Should an admin be stopped from changing their *own* account's plan, not only from giving themselves credits?** Today an admin can change their own plan, end dates and plan exceptions from the admin tools — including raising their own monthly credits through an exception. Slice 11 will stop an admin giving credits to their own login (that is already required). The question is whether the same rule should cover the seven existing plan changes. **Yes:** an admin's own account can only be changed by the other admin; this also applies to the account an admin tests with, if it is their own login (test accounts on a different login are unaffected). **No:** admins keep changing their own plan as today, and the gap stays recorded as a known issue. SA recommends **yes** (a self-granted exception is a self-grant by another name), at about a quarter of a day in 11b. Nothing is enforced yet, so neither answer changes what any customer experiences today.

#### Conditions (S11-C-n)

1. **BA, before the 11a workplan:** fold in S11-CR-1 to S11-CR-4 and the rulings; move S11-D-6 out of the user list (ruled A); correct S11-AC-6 (no combined remaining; plan left, extra and "over the plan" figures), S11-AC-2 (replay writes no audit row, via the outcome), S11-AC-9 (platform account; payment hold fail-closed); add S11-BQ-1; update §18's slice 11 rows; record in the boost requirement: the acknowledgement above, that boost slice 2 creates neither lots table nor draws table, adds no column or FK to them, builds after 20261017 is on PROD, and (under S11-D-1 A) that "Bought credits" becomes "Extra credits".
2. **User, before the 11a workplan:** S11-D-4 (A or C). The other decisions are needed before 11b (D-2, D-3, D-5, BQ-1), 11c (D-8) and 11d (D-1); D-7 is not blocking.

| 2026-09-27 | Created | The last layer of the Business OS LLM standards effort, parked since 2026-09-16. Written as the entitlements programme's S-3 metering seen from the LLM side: 31 FRs, 22 ACs, a five-slice plan, BD-1 to BD-7 and SQ-1 to SQ-10 |
| 2026-09-28 | Currency reopened; BD-1 promoted to a "Decide first" section | The user's framing: the app needs a **currency**, and tier allowances are derived from it — and the 250/500/1,000/2,000 numbers are invented and sold to nobody, so the unit was genuinely open. The user rejected a flat count of AI actions as not cost-linear. Three options set out (price list / live cost / tokens ÷ 10); recorded that "weighted actions" *is* the price-list option, and that `tokens ÷ 10` is not cost-based either — model-blind while Layer 2 lets an operator change the model, and images have no tokens so they would be free. 35 FRs, 25 ACs |
| 2026-09-28 | **BD-1 DECIDED — option B, credits charged at live measured cost** | **One credit is a fixed amount of real provider cost** (~$0.001; the exact value is set with the allowances in D-1b); every action is charged **its own measured cost after it ran**; no prediction, no price list, no re-derivation when a model changes. The user's reason: simpler, and per-action cost cannot be predicted (chat above all), so everything after the fact means no stale price to lose money on and nothing to recalculate. **Knowingly accepted, recorded as KI-1:** the same action varies day to day, and support cannot quote in advance. **New: the credit diary (FR-26)** — a per-action history of time, area, plain-language action, trigger, outcome and credits, which reconciles with the balance and carries no prompt, owner text, model output, tokens or dollars; it ships with the card in **D-2**, because under option B it *is* the owner's predictability. **FR-27** adds a typical range drawn from the owner's own history, labelled as past spend and never a price. The four approved rules specified: **FR-12** tightened (loud failure, a conservative documented fallback rather than a zero charge, a flagged row, operator surfacing, reconciliation by adjustment); **FR-6** rounding (fractional storage, rounded display, diary reconciles with balance); **FR-8 / FR-9** failed and retried actions charged what was actually spent, with idempotency protecting against double-recording the same spend rather than against charging real spend; **FR-5** fan-out needs no multiplier and the pre-check estimate is never the charge. §2 rewritten as resolved, with the option table kept for the record and short reasons why A and C were not chosen. SQ-2, SQ-3 and SQ-4 updated for the "price not knowable in advance" consequence; **SQ-8** (rounding precision and the reconciliation rule) and **SQ-9** (what "conservative" means) added. D-1 now measures to set the **credit value**, not a price list. Now **38 FRs, 28 ACs**; BD-4 settled by BD-1; Q1, Q6 and OQ-7 closed |
